1	# CRD (Cross-Representation Disagreement) 实验审计报告

5	---
6	
7	## 1. 代码结构
8	
9	路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/crdd/`
10	
11	| 文件 | 功能 |
12	|------|------|
13	| `src/config.py` | API/路径/数据集配置 |
14	| `src/data_loader.py` | FOLIO 数据集加载器（本地 JSONL） |
15	| `src/formalize.py` | 双表征生成器：FOL→Z3 代码 + Python constraint 代码（两个独立 prompt，无信息泄漏） |
16	| `src/executor.py` | 代码沙箱执行（超时30s） |
17	| `src/disagree.py` | 分歧检测 + rule-based 错误分类器（9种错误类型） |
18	| `src/repair.py` | 定向修复（带诊断信息）+ 盲目重生成（对照组） |
19	| `src/pipeline.py` | 主 pipeline：加载 → 双表征 → 执行 → 分歧 → 错误分类 → 修复，支持断点续跑 |
20	| `src/baseline.py` | Qwen3-32B 直接回答（无双表征），确认 baseline acc ≤ 85% |
21	| `src/evaluate_mvp.py` | C1-C5 MVP gate 评估 |
22	| `src/evaluate_unified.py` | 统一评估脚本（含 bootstrap CI + Fisher exact test） |
23	| `src/deep_analysis.py` | 深度分析：错误分布、GT子集、分歧不对称性 |
24	| `src/analysis_supplement_ef.py` | 补充分析：phi 相关系数 + 成本效益分析 |
25	| `src/gt_true_case_study.py` | GT=True 子集中定向修复失败的 case study |
26	| `src/repair_only.py` | 单独重跑修复阶段 |
27	| `src/math_adapter.py` | MATH Level 5 数据集适配器 |
28	| `src/proofwriter_adapter.py` | ProofWriter 数据集适配器 |
29	| `src/run_proofwriter_pilot.py` | ProofWriter 试点运行脚本 |
30	| `src/self_consistency_baseline.py` | Self-consistency baseline（k=5 majority vote） |
31	| `analyze_crashes*.py` | 崩溃样本分析脚本 |
32	| `c4_analysis*.py` | C4 指标深度分析 |
33	
34	## 2. 方法概述（CRD vs SICA 差异）
35	
36	**CRD（Cross-Representation Disagreement Diagnostics）**：
37	- 核心思想：同一推理问题用两种**独立表征**（FOL/Z3 + Python constraint code）分别形式化并执行
38	- 两路结果不一致 → 触发分歧检测 → 错误分类 → 定向修复
39	- 关键假设：两种表征的错误模式相互独立（phi=0.189，弱相关，验证通过）
40	- 目标：通过跨表征对比实现**无需 ground truth 的错误定位和修复**
41	
42	**SICA（Symbolic Invariant Consensus Architecture）**：
43	- 项目要求的正式方法，使用符号不变量共识机制
44	- 与 CRD 的核心差异待 SICA 实现后对比
45	
46	## 3. 已完成实验列表
47	
48	### 3.1 Baseline（直接回答）
49	- **模型**: Qwen3-32B-AWQ（本地 vLLM）
50	- **数据集**: FOLIO validation, 50 题
51	- **准确率**: 74.0% (37/50)
52	- **结论**: ≤ 85%，数据集错误率充足，PASS
53	- **运行时间**: 2026-05-14 15:11–15:12
54	
55	### 3.2 Pipeline v1（初始83题）
56	- **模型**: Qwen3-32B-AWQ
57	- **数据集**: FOLIO validation, 83 题
58	- **运行时间**: 2026-05-14
59	- **关键指标**:
60	  - 分歧率 C1: 30.1% (25/83), 有效分歧率 32.1% (25/78)
61	  - 诊断率 C2: 88.0% (22/25)
62	  - 错误类型 C3: 5 种 (negation_error, logic_error, exec_crash, quantifier_error, unknown)
63	  - 定向修复优势 C4: +21.9pp (T:75% vs B:53.1%)
64	  - 定位准确率 C5: 44.4% (原始, 有 bug) → 80.0% (修正后)
65	  - FOL 准确率: 81.9%, Code 准确率: 62.7%, Oracle: 87.9%
66	- **MVP Gate**: 5/5 PASS
67	
68	### 3.3 Pipeline v2 & v3（10题试点）
69	- **v2 (10题)**: 分歧率 0%, 修复率 T:80% B:40%, FOL acc=90%, Code acc=60%
70	- **v3 (10题)**: 分歧率 0%, FOL acc=20%, Code acc=60%
71	- 小样本试运行，结果不稳定
72	
73	### 3.4 Pipeline v4 Full（211题，主实验）
74	- **模型**: Qwen3-32B-AWQ
75	- **数据集**: FOLIO validation, 全部 211 题（去重后 204 题有效）
76	- **运行时间**: 2026-05-14 ~ 2026-05-15（含 resume 和 repair 重跑）
77	- **关键指标 (all, N=204)**:
78	  - 分歧率 C1: 42.2% [95% CI: 35.3%–49.0%]
79	  - 诊断率 C2: 87.2% [79.7%–93.9%]
80	  - 错误类型 C3: 4 种 (exec_crash, logic_error, negation_error, quantifier_error)
81	  - 定向修复优势 C4: +4.7pp (T:69.8% B:65.1%), Fisher p=0.63 (不显著)
82	  - 定位准确率 C5(committed): 94.6% (35/37), C5(coverage): 53.0% (35/66)
83	- **关键指标 (excl Unknown, N=135)**:
84	  - C1: 38.5%, C2: 84.6%, C3: 3种, C4: -1.9pp (不显著), C5(committed): 96.2%
85	- **MVP Gate (all)**: 5/5 PASS
86	- **分歧不对称性**: FOL 正确 & Code 错 = 56, Code 正确 & FOL 错 = 10 (5.6:1)
87	- **准确率**: FOL=78.9%, Code=59.8%, Oracle=87.8%, Pipeline(repair+fallback)=81.4%
88	
89	### 3.5 GT 子集分析
90	| 子集 | N | 分歧率 | 诊断率 | 定向优势 |
91	|------|---|--------|--------|----------|
92	| GT=True | 72 | 30.6% | 90.9% | -13.6pp |
93	| GT=False | 63 | 47.6% | 80.0% | +6.7pp |
94	| GT=Unknown | 69 | 49.3% | 91.2% | +14.7pp |
95	
96	### 3.6 成本效益分析
97	- Token 总量: 3,543,447 tokens, 1,196 API calls
98	- 每样本平均: 17,370 tokens, 5.9 calls
99	- 成本倍数: 全 pipeline / 单表征 = 5.76x
100	- 收益: +21.6% 准确率提升 (vs Code-only baseline)
101	
102	### 3.7 ProofWriter 试点（进行中）
103	- **数据集**: ProofWriter OWA-D5, 30 题 pilot
104	- **状态**: 已完成 28/30 条（pipeline.log 仍在运行中）
105	- **运行时间**: 2026-05-15
106	
107	## 4. C1-C5 MVP Gate 定义
108	
109	| Gate | 名称 | 公式 | 阈值 |
110	|------|------|------|------|
111	| C1 | Disagree Rate | FOL判断 ≠ Code判断 / N | ≥10% |
112	| C2 | Diagnostic Rate | (≥1方正确) / n_disagree | ≥40% |
113	| C3 | Error Type Diversity | 不同错误类型数 | ≥2 |
114	| C4 | Targeted vs Blind | targeted_rate − blind_rate | >5pp |
115	| C5 | Localization Acc | stability.predicted == GT-actual | ≥60% |
116	
117	## 5. 已知问题
118	
119	1. **C4 统计不显著**: Fisher exact test p=0.63，定向修复优势在全集上无统计显著性
120	2. **C4 在 excl_unknown 上为负**: -1.9pp，说明对 GT=True/False 样本，定向修复反而略差于盲修
121	3. **C5 bug 修复历史**: 原始 C5 计算存在循环论证（predicted 和 actual 都从 GT 派生），已修复为用 stability check
122	4. **GT=True 子集定向修复效果差**: 定向优势 -13.6pp，case study 分析表明 GT=True 时 FOL 表征过于自信
123	
124	## 6. 值得保留的数据
125	
126	| 文件 | 内容 | 保留价值 |
127	|------|------|----------|
128	| `results/mvp_v4_full.jsonl` | 204 条完整 pipeline 结果 | 高（主实验原始数据） |
129	| `results/eval_unified_folio_d012.json` | 统一评估含 bootstrap CI | 高（统计分析） |
130	| `results/exp001_deep_analysis.json/txt` | 错误分布+GT子集分析 | 高（论文素材） |
131	| `results/exp001_supplement_ef.json/txt` | phi系数+成本效益 | 高（论文素材） |
132	| `results/baseline.json` | 50 题 baseline 原始结果 | 中 |
133	| `results/proofwriter_pilot.jsonl` | ProofWriter 28 条结果 | 中（跨数据集泛化） |
134	| `artifacts/c1c5_audit.md` | C5 bug 修复审计 | 中（方法论记录） |

Perfect! Now let me compile my comprehensive findings:

## COMPREHENSIVE CRITICAL ISSUES REPORT

Based on a thorough search of all LaTeX source files in `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/`, I found the following critical issues:

### CATEGORY 2: INTERNAL INFO LEAK

**1 CRITICAL ISSUE FOUND:**

**File:** `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/appendix.tex`
**Line:** 568
**Content:** `...pattern robust to D064 correction.}`
**Issue:** References an internal decision ID "D064" which appears to be from the project infrastructure tracking system. This is an internal artifact reference that should not appear in a submission-ready paper. The phrase "D064 correction" suggests this is a specific internal decision or bugfix identifier.

**Full context:**
```
\caption{Entropy decomposition of SICA $\Delta$ (pp). High-entropy: majority count $\leq 8$; 
low-entropy: majority count $\geq 9$. $^\dagger$Partial data (180/600). Pre-tiebreaking SC 
values; pattern robust to D064 correction.}
```

### CATEGORY 1: PLACEHOLDERS
**Result:** None found. The paper contains no TODO, FIXME, XXX, TBD, PLACEHOLDER, "??", "[REF]", or "[CITE]" markers.

### CATEGORY 3: NUMBER INCONSISTENCIES
**Result:** All major numerical claims are consistent across abstract/conclusion/introduction with the experiments section:
- "six models" → Correct (6 main models: Qwen2.5-14B, Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3-14B, Mistral-7B, LLaMA-3.1-8B, QwQ-32B)
- "four reasoning benchmarks" → Correct (FOLIO, ProofWriter, LogiQA, StrategyQA as main; GSM8K and AIME are supplementary)
- "over 1,200 instances" → Correct (total: 204+600+200+200+30+22 = 1,256 instances)
- "bias ratios $1.66$--$4.97\times$ across nine models" → Correct (documented in appendix Table ~tab:frontier-scb)
- "+6.67pp" cross-architecture gain → Correct (ProofWriter Mistral-7B + DeBERTa NLI)
- "twenty-seven training-free variants" → Correct (mentioned in introduction line 13 and conclusion)
- Oracle controls (+29.90/+13.73/+17.17pp) → All confirmed in experiments and appendix

### CATEGORY 4: NARRATIVE DRIFT / TERMINOLOGY
**Result:** Terminology is consistent throughout. Key terms are used uniformly:
- "symbolic confirmation bias" (SCB)
- "symbolic mirage"
- "persistent empirical property"
- "trace-level" vs "collection-level" distinctions are clear
- No inconsistent usage of conceptual terminology found

### SUMMARY

**Total Critical Issues: 1**

The paper has one critical issue that must be fixed before submission: the reference to "D064 correction" on line 568 of appendix.tex is an internal infrastructure identifier that leaks project artifact tracking. This should be either:
1. Removed entirely and the caption revised to remove the reference
2. Replaced with a descriptive explanation of what the correction entails (if it's important to document)

All other critical categories passed inspection.

I'm leaning toward adding Figure 7 to the main text since it represents a key insight.

Now I'm mapping out the figure improvements needed: redesigning Figures 1 and 3 for consistency and quality, moving Figure 7 from the appendix into the main narrative, condensing Figure 4, and ensuring all figures use a unified color palette. Let me check what Figure 7 currently looks like.

{
  "text": "用户要求对正文图表做全面优化。以下任务按优先级排列，全部完成：\n\n**任务 1：Fig 1 和 Fig 3 用 PaperBanana 重新画（最高优先级）**\n\n两张图之前的 PaperBanana 版本用户不满意，这次必须大幅提升质量。要求：\n\nFig 1（SICA Pipeline）：\n- 全宽 figure*，水平流程图\n- 设计参考：顶会论文的高质量 pipeline 图风格——清晰的模块化布局、渐变色块背景、精致的图标/符号\n- 核心流程：Question → Sample K CoT Traces → Extract FOL Constraints → Dedup → Weight (MAX-SAT Z3) → Answer\n- 用红色虚线框标注 \"Independence Bottleneck\"（从 trace 到 constraint 的环节）\n- 配色专业统一，有层次感（深蓝/紫/青等学术色调），不要太花哨\n- 每个模块内部有简短说明文字\n- 给 PaperBanana 的 prompt 要非常详细，描述每个元素的位置、颜色、大小、字体\n\nFig 3（Causal DAG）：\n- 单栏 figure，但图片本身要精美\n- M → T → A → C → S 因果链，Y 作为虚线连接\n- 与 Fig 1 同一配色体系\n- 节点要大、标签清晰、有阴影/立体感\n- A→C 用红色粗箭头标注 bottleneck\n- 增加 \"Generation\" 和 \"Verification\" 背景分组\n- C ⊥⊥ Y | A 用灰色注释\n\n**每张图至少迭代 3 轮**，不满意就继续生成。质量标准：看起来像专业设计师画的，不像 AI 随手生成的。\n\n**任务 2：将附录 Figure 7（Independence-Accuracy Trade-off）提升到正文**\n\n这张散点图直接支撑论文的核心贡献之一（independence-accuracy trade-off），放在附录太浪费。\n\n操作：\n1. 将 Figure 7 的定义从 appendix.tex 移到主文（放在 Section 5 \"Diagnostic Framework\" 之后或 Section 6 开头附近）\n2. 改为单栏 figure\n3. 更新所有 \\ref{fig:...} 引用\n4. 确保 figure 编号自动更新（appendix 中后续图重新编号）\n\n**任务 3：Figure 4（27 策略条形图）缩小**\n\n当前全宽 figure* 占地太大。改为：\n- 保持 figure*（全宽），但缩小整体图幅高度\n- 字体从当前大小缩小约 20-30%\n- 条形间距压缩\n- 或者只展示最有代表性的 15-20 个策略（删掉效果接近的冗余策略），减少条目数\n\n**任务 4：编译 + 页数检查**\n\n全部修改后：\n1. 编译确认 0 error\n2. 正文仍 ≤ 8 页（加了 Figure 7 后如果超页，需要压缩文字）\n3. 汇报新的 figure 编号和位置\n\n**并行执行**：任务 1 的 Fig 1 和 Fig 3 可以用两个 Worker 并行画。任务 2/3 可以另一个 Worker 同时处理 tex 修改。",

_source line 671 · 2026-05-24T17:33:49.149Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01H2NcFa8omHB7mLRyRbkrun`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}\label{sec:introduction}
2	
3	\looseness=-1
4	Self-Consistency (SC) \citep{wang2023sc} selects the majority answer across $K$ chain-of-thought traces, a strategy grounded in the Condorcet Jury Theorem: independent voters converge to truth \citep{condorcet1785, ladha1992correlated}. In practice, SC's gains plateau for strong models \citep{loo2025reevaluating}, pointing to correlated traces that reduce the effective ensemble size. A natural remedy is to move beyond simple vote-counting: extract logical constraints from the traces and let a provably sound solver adjudicate. If the solver can filter contradictions and enforce logical consistency, it should recover information that raw majority voting discards.
5	
6	\looseness=-1
7	We test this intuition through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from reasoning traces (prompted as first-order logic; 99\% are propositional at the Z3 level) and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT (\S\ref{sec:pipeline}; Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline}). Despite Z3 validating nearly all self-extracted constraints, SICA produces answers identical to majority voting on the vast majority of problems. Across six models (7B--32B), four benchmarks, and multi-seed replication, no condition shows a significant positive effect after FDR control (Table~\ref{tab:main_results}). Four of six powered conditions confirm equivalence within $\pm$2\,pp (TOST); the remainder are underpowered for smaller effects.
8	
9	\looseness=-1
10	The failure is not in the solver but in the constraints it operates on. Self-extracted constraints systematically support the model's majority answer---a robust empirical regularity of zero-shot self-extraction pipelines we term \emph{symbolic confirmation bias} (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Because each trace determines both an answer and its constraints, the formal channel reduces to a weighted echo of the majority vote. We show this degeneration is robust (Observation~1) and confirm it holds across nine models from 7B to frontier scale. Prior work shows LLMs cannot self-correct reasoning without external feedback \citep{huang2024selfcorrect}; our analysis reveals a deeper mechanism underlying these findings, providing a Markov chain formalization and actionable diagnostics for self-verification.
11	
12	\looseness=-1
13	Twenty-seven training-free variants within the constraint-extraction paradigm confirm the trade-off is fundamental (\S\ref{sec:remediation}): methods that break trace correlation destroy accuracy, while methods that preserve accuracy fail to achieve independence. Cross-architecture verification provides preliminary evidence of escape: a structurally independent verifier yields significant gains when it exceeds the generator's accuracy, with benefit governed by a capability-gap pattern between verifier and generator (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}). Three zero-cost diagnostics enable practitioners to predict a priori whether any self-extraction-based aggregation method will help (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).
14	
15	Our contributions are:
16	\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
17	\item \textbf{The symbolic mirage.} Provably sound Z3 verification becomes vacuous under self-extraction. We diagnose the underlying mechanism: self-extracted constraints inherit the generating model's distributional prior (symbolic confirmation bias), reducing SICA to majority vote under source-attribution scoring (Observation~1). The pattern holds across nine models and 27 training-free variants (\S\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}, \S\ref{sec:remediation}).\footnote{Code and data will be released upon acceptance.}
18	
19	\item \textbf{Diagnostic framework.} Three zero-cost diagnostics characterize failure modes across all tested conditions within the self-extraction paradigm, with prospective validation on held-out model families, providing practitioners with a priori failure-mode diagnosis from a pilot run (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).
20	
21	\item \textbf{Cross-architecture escape.} Preliminary evidence that structurally independent verification can break the bottleneck, with gain governed by a capability-gap pattern between verifier and generator (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}).
22	\end{enumerate}
23	
24	\begin{figure*}[t]
25	\centering
26	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figures/fig1_pipeline_pb_v6.pdf}
27	\caption{The SICA pipeline. SC selects the majority answer from $K$ traces. SICA extracts logical constraints, deduplicates, and solves MAX-SAT via Z3. Red dashed box: the independence bottleneck. Dashed arrow: oracle variant with gold FOL.}
28	\label{fig:pipeline}
29	\end{figure*}
30	
```

      "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
      "content": "\\section{Introduction}\\label{sec:introduction}\n\n\\looseness=-1\nSelf-Consistency (SC) \\citep{wang2023sc} selects the majority answer across $K$ chain-of-thought traces, a strategy grounded in the Condorcet Jury Theorem: independent voters converge to truth \\citep{condorcet1785, ladha1992correlated}. In practice, SC's gains plateau for strong models \\citep{loo2025reevaluating}, pointing to correlated traces that reduce the effective ensemble size. A natural remedy is to move beyond simple vote-counting: extract logical constraints from the traces and let a provably sound solver adjudicate. If the solver can filter contradictions and enforce logical consistency, it should recover information that raw majority voting discards.\n\n\\looseness=-1\nWe test this intuition through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from reasoning traces (prompted as first-order logic; 99\\% are propositional at the Z3 level) and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT (\\S\\ref{sec:pipeline}; Figure~\\ref{fig:pipeline}). Despite Z3 validating nearly all self-extracted constraints, SICA produces answers identical to majority voting on the vast majority of problems. Across six models (7B--32B), four benchmarks, and multi-seed replication, no condition shows a significant positive effect after FDR control (Table~\\ref{tab:main_results}). Four of six powered conditions confirm equivalence within $\\pm$2\\,pp (TOST); the remainder are underpowered for smaller effects.\n\n\\looseness=-1\nThe failure is not in the solver but in the constraints it operates on. Self-extracted constraints systematically support the model's majority answer---a robust empirical regularity of zero-shot self-extraction pipelines we term \\emph{symbolic confirmation bias} (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}). Because each trace determines both an answer and its constraints, the formal channel reduces to a weighted echo of the majority vote. We show this degeneration is robust (Observation~1) and confirm it holds across nine models from 7B to frontier scale. Prior work shows LLMs cannot self-correct reasoning without external feedback \\citep{huang2024selfcorrect}; our analysis reveals a deeper mechanism underlying these findings, providing a Markov chain formalization and actionable diagnostics for self-verification.\n\n\\looseness=-1\nTwenty-seven training-free variants within the constraint-extraction paradigm confirm the trade-off is fundamental (\\S\\ref{sec:remediation}): methods that break trace correlation destroy accuracy, while methods that preserve accuracy fail to achieve independence. Cross-architecture verification provides preliminary evidence of escape: a structurally independent verifier yields significant gains when it exceeds the generator's accuracy, with benefit governed by a capability-gap pattern between verifier and generator (\\S\\ref{sec:cross-arch}). Three zero-cost diagnostics enable practitioners to predict a priori whether any self-extraction-based aggregation method will help (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).\n\nOur contributions are:\n\\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]\n\\item \\textbf{The symbolic mirage.} Provably sound Z3 verification becomes vacuous under self-extraction. We diagnose the underlying mechanism: self-extracted constraints inherit the generating model's distributional prior (symbolic confirmation bias), reducing SICA to majority vote under source-attribution scoring (Observation~1). The pattern holds across nine models and 27 training-free variants (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}, \\S\\ref{sec:remediation}).\\footnote{Code and data will be released upon acceptance.}\n\n\\item \\textbf{Diagnostic framework.} Three zero-cost diagnostics characterize failure modes across all tested conditions within the self-extraction paradigm, with prospective validation on held-out model families, providing practitioners with a priori failure-mode diagnosis from a pilot run (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).\n\n\\item \\textbf{Cross-architecture escape.} Preliminary evidence that structurally independent verification can break the bottleneck, with gain governed by a capability-gap pattern between verifier and generator (\\S\\ref{sec:cross-arch}).\n\\end{enumerate}\n\n\\begin{figure*}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\textwidth]{figures/fig1_pipeline_pb_v6.pdf}\n\\caption{The SICA pipeline. SC selects the majority answer from $K$ traces. SICA extracts logical constraints, deduplicates, and solves MAX-SAT via Z3. Red dashed box: the independence bottleneck. Dashed arrow: oracle variant with gold FOL.}\n\\label{fig:pipeline}\n\\end{figure*}\n",
      "numLines": 30,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 30
    }
  },

}

```text
1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	\label{sec:conclusion}
4	
5	\looseness=-1
6	Our central finding is that the value of formal verification depends on the \emph{independence} of its inputs, not the soundness of its logic. \emph{SICA-specific}: under source-attribution scoring, SICA reduces to majority vote (Observation~1), and 27 training-free variants fail to escape. \emph{General}: the DPI bound applies to any deterministic self-extraction, and symbolic confirmation bias---a persistent empirical pattern across all tested zero-shot self-extraction conditions, not a cognitive bias analogy---holds across nine models (7B to frontier), suggesting the bottleneck is intrinsic to self-referential constraint generation. Three diagnostics ($K_\text{eff}$, BR, $C_0$) correctly characterize failure modes across all tested conditions, with boundaries generalizing to held-out families (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).
7	
8	\looseness=-1
9	These results connect to ensemble diversity theory \citep{dietterich2000ensemble, krogh1994neural}: with $K_\text{eff} \leq 3.94$ for $K{=}12$, same-model traces scale redundancy, not information. The effect is capability-modulated: Qwen3-14B on ProofWriter produces the most redundant non-thinking traces ($\kappa = 0.78$, $K_{\text{eff}} = 1.25$) yet $|\Delta|$ remains small across four seeds (mean $+0.92$\,pp, $\sigma = 1.14$, n.s.), consistent with $K_{\text{eff}} = 1.25$ limiting the corrective capacity of constraint-based consensus when the SC baseline (85.25\%) already minimizes correctable errors. For test-time compute scaling \citep{snell2025scaling}, increasing $K$ within a single model yields diminishing returns when traces share systematic biases.
10	
11	\looseness=-1
12	Escaping the bottleneck requires structural independence (\S\ref{sec:cross-arch}); cross-architecture NLI provides a proof of concept, but whether trained extractors or hybrid approaches can shift the boundary remains open. Our diagnostic framework offers a concrete tool: a $K{=}12$ pilot run with three zero-cost metrics predicts whether any self-extraction method will help before full deployment.
13	
```

```text
1	\looseness=-1 \textbf{Model scale.} Our locally-run models range from 7B to 32B; frontier coverage (gpt-4o, gpt-4.1, gpt-5.5, o3, Gemini-2.5-Pro) relies on cross-model extraction rather than self-extraction, so we cannot confirm that self-extraction produces identical SCB patterns at frontier scale.
2	
3	\looseness=-1 \textbf{Domain scope.} All benchmarks are English logical reasoning. FOLIO and ProofWriter represent a best-case scenario for symbolic verification: problems are designed to have clean first-order logic representations, so the observed bias ratios ($1.66$--$4.97\times$) likely represent a \emph{lower bound} on SCB in less structured domains. We excluded GSM8K and AIME because $C_0 < 5\%$ made SICA degenerate by design; whether symbolic confirmation bias manifests differently in mathematical reasoning with higher extraction rates remains untested.
4	
5	\looseness=-1 \textbf{Statistical power and escape scope.} Statistical power is limited for effects below ${\sim}2$\,pp (MDE $1.86$--$4.84$\,pp across conditions); small positive effects cannot be ruled out. The capability-gap analysis rests on $n{=}6$ rank-correlation conditions (Appendix~\ref{app:limitations-extended}). Multi-seed replication uses $3$--$4$ seeds per model; cross-seed correlation is not modeled, and a larger seed budget could tighten the variance estimates. We do not evaluate trained extractors, which could potentially break the self-extraction bound by introducing external signal.
6	
```

_source line 697 · 2026-05-24T17:37:36.589Z_

[Reviewer 回复]:
## 审查回复 — 正文是否需要加图

**结论**: 不加。

### 分析

**当前密度**：确认正文 4 figures + 2 tables = 6 display items / 8 pages（~0.75/page）。ACL 系论文典型密度 5-8 items，6 是合理水平。

**四张图已覆盖完整叙事链**：
1. Fig1 (pipeline) — 方法概览
2. Fig2 (κ-Δ scatter) — 核心发现：高 κ → Δ≈0
3. Fig3 (causal DAG) — 机制解释：DPI bottleneck
4. Fig4 (κ-accuracy trade-off) — 补救失败：independence-accuracy ceiling

每张图对应论文一个核心 claim，无叙事空白。

**候选图评估**：
- **27 策略条形图**（当前最强候选）：视觉冲击力强，一眼看出"什么都不行"。但 analysis.tex L53 已文字引用 `Figure~\ref{fig:interventions-summary}`（指向 appendix），且 κ-accuracy scatter 已在正文传达同一信息（所有 same-model methods 聚集在 κ≈0.40-0.47 ceiling）。审稿人会看 appendix。
- **K sensitivity**：test-time compute 的次要论点，不值得正文空间。
- **Capability gap**：escape section 已有 Table 支撑，冗余。

**页数风险**：当前刚好 8 页。加一张 figure（~0.3-0.5 page）必须删等量正文。当前每段都在支撑 claim，删减会弱化论证。

**建议**：维持现状。如果审稿人要求补图，R2 阶段提升 27-strategies 图到正文（那时有 rebuttal 空间调整排版）。

[飞书用户 用户]: 📝 论文自检任务：缩写/专有名词定义检查

请对当前论文做一次缩写定义自检。AI 写作常见问题：很多专有名词的缩写（acronym）从第一次出现就没给出全称，整篇论文都默认读者认识。这不符合学术写作规范。

自检要求：

1. 通读全文，列出所有缩写词（包括方法名、数据集名、指标名、模型名）
2. 对每个缩写，确认它在【第一次出现时】用了 "全称 (缩写)" 格式
   ✅ 正确：Large Language Model (LLM) ... 后续可直接用 LLM
   ❌ 错误：摘要里用 LLM，但全文从未给出 Large Language Model
3. 特别关注以下高发区：
   - 你自己造的方法名/算法名（例如 SCALE、TRACE、PIVOT 这类）——是首字母缩写还是单纯命名？如果是缩写，必须说明展开
   - 数据集/benchmark 名（MMLU、ARC、GSM8K 等常见 benchmark 也要在首次出现时给全称）
   - 指标缩写（F1、AUC、BLEU、ROUGE-L 等）
   - 领域术语（RLHF、DPO、CoT、ICL、PEFT 等）
4. Abstract 和 Introduction 是首次出现的高发区，Abstract 里的缩写也要在 Abstract 里展开一次

修完后请简短汇报修了哪些。

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
I can see the current versions. Looking at them:

**Fig 1 (p3)**: The pipeline diagram shows boxes connected by arrows in a horizontal flow. The boxes contain:
- Question (q)
- Sample K CoT Traces
- Extract FOL Constraints (vs P(c)→Q(a))
- Dedup + Weight
- MAX-SAT (Z3)
- Answer (a*)

There's also a "Majority Vote" branch and an "SC" label. The "Independence Bottleneck" is marked with a red dashed box. There's also "oracle: gold FOL" text at the top.


The design needs significant visual improvement—the boxes are too basic with thin borders, the color palette is muted, text is small and hard to read, and there's no professional polish like gradients, shadows, or icons. **Fig 3 (p7)** shows a causal DAG with nodes M, T, A, C, S in a chain and Y separate, with different node colors but still lacking visual refinement. The user has iterated through multiple versions already.

I think the core problem is that PaperBanana isn't producing publication-quality figures that match what they're looking for. I should try a different strategy—either dive deeper into the PaperBanana skill to understand how to craft better prompts with specific visual design references, or explore using matplotlib directly for more control over the styling.
```

13	## 叙事类型分布
14	
15	**突破局限型最主流**（5/11：Aristotle, CREST, Khan, SatLM, SymbCoT）——先暴露现有方法缺陷再提出修复。**统一框架型**（2/11：DyLoS, ProverQA）。**跨界迁移型**（2/11：LINC, Logic-LM）。**化繁为简型**（2/11：Dissecting, xiong2024）。
16	
17	## Intro模式
18	
19	**4-5段标准**（9/11，高）：(1)领域→(2)局限→(3)方案→(4)贡献→[可选(5)结构]。SymbCoT/Aristotle例外用6段（多CoT背景或root cause分析段）。
20	
21	**3条贡献标配**（8/11，高）。变体：LINC 2条；Khan 9条numbered insights；SatLM "2 advantages + 3 findings"。
22	
23	**贡献嵌数字**（3/11，中）：DyLoS("+27%")、SatLM("+23%")、SymbCoT("+7.88%")——Intro即有说服力。
24	
25	## 可模仿技巧（按实用性排序）
26	
27	1. **范式对比图**（SatLM Fig1, SymbCoT Fig1）：side-by-side对比旧方法(错误标红)vs新方法(正确标绿)。problem_illustration类Fig1最主流(3/11)。
28	
29	2. **角色重构**（3/11）："LLM is translator not solver"(Logic-LM)；"declarative not imperative"(SatLM)。一句重定义格局。
30	
31	3. **Parse-Plan-Execute分解**（SatLM）：展示offload步骤数(ProgLM 1/3 vs SatLM 2/3)——通用于hybrid系统。
32	
33	4. **对手优势反转**（SymbCoT）：外部solver的reliability重构为"inflexibility+information loss"。
34	
35	5. **Teaser result图放Intro**（Aristotle）：Fig1 bar chart量化问题，未看方法已知需求。
36	
37	6. **诊断baseline**（LINC Scratchpad, SatLM CoTSolver）：中间态baseline隔离组件因果贡献。
38	
39	7. **Mirror figure pair**（Khan Fig3-4）：同指标两设置相反趋势，视觉对称强化论证。
40	
41	8. **Design space分解**（xiong2024）：empirical study组织为prompting×sampling×aggregation三维框架。
42	
43	## Figure编排
44	
45	**Fig1类型**：method_overview(7/11)最多；problem_illustration(3/11)其次；teaser_result(1/11)。

1	---
2	description: 用 PaperBanana 生成高品质学术概念图（架构/流程/framework）。统计图不要用 PaperBanana，用 draw-paper-figure。需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。

16	
17	# Paper Banana — 高品质学术概念图生成
18	
19	PaperBanana 由 Gemini Pro 自动写 matplotlib 代码，VLM Critic 评估渲染结果并迭代优化，输出顶会级别 300 DPI 概念图。
20	
21	**质量底线：每张图必须达到顶会论文的美观度——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。**
22	
23	## 适用范围
24	
25	| 场景 | 用什么 |
26	|------|--------|
27	| **概念图**（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana** ← 本 skill |
28	| **统计图**（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md**。**不要用 PaperBanana 画统计图** |
29	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md** |
30	
31	## 执行环境
32	
33	**PaperBanana 在本机（EC2）运行，不要 SSH 到远程服务器。**
34	
35	EC2（ap-northeast-1 东京）可直接访问 Gemini API，无需代理。
36	
37	## 前置条件
38	
39	### Step 1：获取 GOOGLE_API_KEY
40	
41	```python
42	request_secret(key_id="google_api_key", project="&lt;project_name>", purpose="PaperBanana 概念图生成")
43	# 等待 Director 审批后
44	poll_secret_request(request_id="<返回的 request_id>", project="&lt;project_name>")
45	```
46	
47	如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
48	
49	### Step 2：安装验证（每次使用前必须检查）
50	
51	```bash
52	# 检查是否已安装
53	paperbanana --version 2>/dev/null || {
54	  echo "PaperBanana 未安装，正在安装..."
55	  pip install paperbanana -q
56	  paperbanana --version
57	}
58	```
59	
60	如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。
61	
62	**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 PaperBanana。
63	
64	## 命令：generate
65	
66	概念图用 `generate` 子命令。**必须显式指定 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro`**（默认模型 `gemini-2.0-flash` 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。
67	
68	## 完整流程
69	
70	### Step 1：写意图描述文件
71	
72	将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：
73	
74	```bash
75	cat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'
76	Three-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):
77	
78	Panel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):
79	Grouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).
80	Exact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...
````

122	### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）
123	
124	**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：

126	1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？
127	2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？
128	3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？
129	4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？
130	5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？

135	
136	```bash

139	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg
140	img = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')

143	ax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')
144	plt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)
145	plt.close()
146	"
147	```

149	## 意图描述质量红线

151	Intent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：

154	2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)
155	3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说"蓝色"，说 "#2C3E50"，绑定语义
156	4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation
157	5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels
158	6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度

   - EMNLP 2026 ARR formatting rules: 8-page main text limit, 200-word abstract, Limitations section doesn't count toward page limit
   - LaTeX figure environments: `figure` (single column) vs `figure*` (double column/full width)

    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/limitations_content.tex",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/limitations_content.tex",
        "content": "\\looseness=-1 Our locally-run models range from 7B to 32B; frontier coverage relies on cross-model extraction, so we cannot confirm identical SCB patterns at frontier scale. All benchmarks are English logical reasoning; FOLIO and ProofWriter represent a best-case for symbolic verification, and the observed bias ratios ($1.66$--$4.97\\times$) likely represent a \\emph{lower bound} on SCB in less structured domains. Statistical power is limited for effects below ${\\sim}2$\\,pp (MDE $1.86$--$4.84$\\,pp across conditions); small positive effects cannot be ruled out. We do not evaluate trained extractors, which could potentially break the self-extraction bound by introducing external signal.\n",
        "numLines": 2,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 2
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/limitations_content.tex"
  },

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/discussion.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Discussion and Conclusion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\n\\looseness=-1\nOur central finding is that formal verification's value depends on the \\emph{independence} of its inputs, not the soundness of its logic. SICA reduces to majority vote under source-attribution scoring (Observation~1), and 27 training-free variants fail to escape---the DPI bound applies to any deterministic self-extraction, and symbolic confirmation bias holds across nine models (7B to frontier). With $K_\\text{eff} \\leq 3.94$ for $K{=}12$ \\citep{dietterich2000ensemble}, same-model traces scale redundancy, not information \\citep{snell2025scaling}. Three diagnostics characterize failure modes across all tested conditions (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}), and escaping the bottleneck requires structural independence (\\S\\ref{sec:cross-arch}).\n",
        "numLines": 7,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 7
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/discussion.tex"

```json
{
  "parentUuid": "5711e389-85b6-43d2-a224-6a58f3bf4c6e",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Introduction}\\label{sec:introduction}\n\n\\looseness=-1\nSelf-Consistency (SC) \\citep{wang2023sc} selects the majority answer across $K$ chain-of-thought traces, a strategy grounded in the Condorcet Jury Theorem: independent voters converge to truth \\citep{condorcet1785, ladha1992correlated}. In practice, SC's gains plateau for strong models \\citep{loo2025reevaluating, byerly2025selfconsistency}, and correlated LLM errors \\citep{kim2025correlated} reduce the effective ensemble size \\citep{dietterich2000ensemble}. A natural remedy is to move beyond simple vote-counting: extract logical constraints from the traces---as in neuro-symbolic approaches \\citep{pan2023logiclm, olausson2023linc, ye2024satlm}---and let a provably sound solver adjudicate. If the solver can filter contradictions and enforce logical consistency, it should recover information that raw majority voting discards.\n\n\\looseness=-1\nWe test this intuition through Symbolic Invariant Consensus Aggregation (SICA), which extracts logical constraints from reasoning traces (prompted as first-order logic (FOL); 99\\% are propositional at the level of the Z3 SMT solver) and selects answers via weighted partial MAX-SAT (\\S\\ref{sec:pipeline}; Figure~\\ref{fig:pipeline}). Despite Z3 validating nearly all self-extracted constraints \\citep[cf.][]{chan2026position}, SICA produces answers identical to majority voting on the vast majority of problems. Across six models (7B--32B), four benchmarks, and multi-seed replication, no condition shows a significant positive effect after FDR control (Table~\\ref{tab:main_results}). Four of six powered conditions confirm equivalence within $\\pm$2\\,pp (Two One-Sided Tests; TOST); the remainder are underpowered for smaller effects.\n\n\\looseness=-1\nThe failure is not in the solver but in the constraints it operates on. Self-extracted constraints systematically support the model's majority answer---a robust empirical regularity of zero-shot self-extraction pipelines we term \\emph{symbolic confirmation bias} (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}), distinct from trace-level unfaithfulness \\citep{turpin2023language, wan2025unveiling}. Because each trace determines both an answer and its constraints, the formal channel reduces to a weighted echo of the majority vote. We show this degeneration is robust (Observation~1) and confirm it holds across nine models from 7B to frontier scale. Prior work shows LLMs cannot self-correct reasoning without external feedback \\citep{huang2024selfcorrect, kamoi2024selfcorrect}; our analysis reveals a deeper mechanism underlying these findings, providing a Markov chain formalization and actionable diagnostics for self-verification.\n\n\\looseness=-1\nTwenty-seven training-free variants within the constraint-extraction paradigm confirm the trade-off is fundamental (\\S\\ref{sec:remediation}): methods that break trace correlation (e.g., multi-agent debate; \\citealp{liang2023encouraging, du2023improving}) destroy accuracy, while methods that preserve accuracy fail to achieve independence---a bottleneck also faced by test-time compute scaling \\citep{snell2025scaling}. Cross-architecture verification provides preliminary evidence of escape: a structurally independent verifier yields significant gains when it exceeds the generator's accuracy, with benefit governed by a capability-gap pattern between verifier and generator (\\S\\ref{sec:cross-arch}). Three zero-cost diagnostics enable practitioners to predict a priori whether any self-extraction-based aggregation method will help (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).\n\nOur contributions are:\n\\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]\n\\item \\textbf{The symbolic mirage.} Provably sound Z3 verification becomes vacuous under self-extraction. We diagnose the underlying mechanism: self-extracted constraints inherit the generating model's distributional prior (symbolic confirmation bias), reducing SICA to majority vote under source-attribution scoring (Observation~1). The pattern holds across nine models and 27 training-free variants (\\S\\ref{sec:confirmation-bias}, \\S\\ref{sec:remediation}).\\footnote{Code and data will be released upon acceptance.}\n\n\\item \\textbf{Diagnostic framework.} Three zero-cost diagnostics characterize failure modes across all tested conditions within the self-extraction paradigm, with prospective validation on held-out model families, providing practitioners with a priori failure-mode diagnosis from a pilot run (\\S\\ref{sec:diagnostic-framework}).\n\n\\item \\textbf{Cross-architecture escape.} Preliminary evidence that structurally independent verification can break the bottleneck, with gain governed by a capability-gap pattern between verifier and generator (\\S\\ref{sec:cross-arch}).\n\\end{enumerate}\n\n\\begin{figure*}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\textwidth]{figures/fig1_pipeline_pb_v6.pdf}\n\\caption{The SICA pipeline. SC selects the majority answer from $K$ traces. SICA extracts logical constraints, deduplicates, and solves MAX-SAT via Z3. Red dashed box: the independence bottleneck. Dashed arrow: oracle variant with gold FOL.}\n\\label{fig:pipeline}\n\\end{figure*}\n",
        "numLines": 30,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 30
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/paper/introduction.tex"
  },
  "type": "attachment",
  "uuid": "9f397b80-358b-4846-9bcf-618dd052bb88",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-24T19:34:27.352Z",

{

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/symb_invariant_consensus/artifacts/crd_audit_report.md",
        "content": "# CRD (Cross-Representation Disagreement) 实验审计报告\n\n**method: CRD** | 审计日期: 2026-05-15 | 服务器: westd-32608\n\n---\n\n## 1. 代码结构\n\n路径: `/root/autodl-tmp/crdd/`\n\n| 文件 | 功能 |\n|------|------|\n| `src/config.py` | API/路径/数据集配置 |\n| `src/data_loader.py` | FOLIO 数据集加载器（本地 JSONL） |\n| `src/formalize.py` | 双表征生成器：FOL→Z3 代码 + Python constraint 代码（两个独立 prompt，无信息泄漏） |\n| `src/executor.py` | 代码沙箱执行（超时30s） |\n| `src/disagree.py` | 分歧检测 + rule-based 错误分类器（9种错误类型） |\n| `src/repair.py` | 定向修复（带诊断信息）+ 盲目重生成（对照组） |\n| `src/pipeline.py` | 主 pipeline：加载 → 双表征 → 执行 → 分歧 → 错误分类 → 修复，支持断点续跑 |\n| `src/baseline.py` | Qwen3-32B 直接回答（无双表征），确认 baseline acc ≤ 85% |\n| `src/evaluate_mvp.py` | C1-C5 MVP gate 评估 |\n| `src/evaluate_unified.py` | 统一评估脚本（含 bootstrap CI + Fisher exact test） |\n| `src/deep_analysis.py` | 深度分析：错误分布、GT子集、分歧不对称性 |\n| `src/analysis_supplement_ef.py` | 补充分析：phi 相关系数 + 成本效益分析 |\n| `src/gt_true_case_study.py` | GT=True 子集中定向修复失败的 case study |\n| `src/repair_only.py` | 单独重跑修复阶段 |\n| `src/math_adapter.py` | MATH Level 5 数据集适配器 |\n| `src/proofwriter_adapter.py` | ProofWriter 数据集适配器 |\n| `src/run_proofwriter_pilot.py` | ProofWriter 试点运行脚本 |\n| `src/self_consistency_baseline.py` | Self-consistency baseline（k=5 majority vote） |\n| `analyze_crashes*.py` | 崩溃样本分析脚本 |\n| `c4_analysis*.py` | C4 指标深度分析 |\n\n## 2. 方法概述（CRD vs SICA 差异）\n\n**CRD（Cross-Representation Disagreement Diagnostics）**：\n- 核心思想：同一推理问题用两种**独立表征**（FOL/Z3 + Python constraint code）分别形式化并执行\n- 两路结果不一致 → 触发分歧检测 → 错误分类 → 定向修复\n- 关键假设：两种表征的错误模式相互独立（phi=0.189，弱相关，验证通过）\n- 目标：通过跨表征对比实现**无需 ground truth 的错误定位和修复**\n\n**SICA（Symbolic Invariant Consensus Architecture）**：\n- 项目要求的正式方法，使用符号不变量共识机制\n- 与 CRD 的核心差异待 SICA 实现后对比\n\n## 3. 已完成实验列表\n\n### 3.1 Baseline（直接回答）\n- **模型**: Qwen3-32B-AWQ（本地 vLLM）\n- **数据集**: FOLIO validation, 50 题\n- **准确率**: 74.0% (37/50)\n- **结论**: ≤ 85%，数据集错误率充足，PASS\n- **运行时间**: 2026-05-14 15:11–15:12\n\n### 3.2 Pipeline v1（初始83题）\n- **模型**: Qwen3-32B-AWQ\n- **数据集**: FOLIO validation, 83 题\n- **运行时间**: 2026-05-14\n- **关键指标**:\n  - 分歧率 C1: 30.1% (25/83), 有效分歧率 32.1% (25/78)\n  - 诊断率 C2: 88.0% (22/25)\n  - 错误类型 C3: 5 种 (negation_error, logic_error, exec_crash, quantifier_error, unknown)\n  - 定向修复优势 C4: +21.9pp (T:75% vs B:53.1%)\n  - 定位准确率 C5: 44.4% (原始, 有 bug) → 80.0% (修正后)\n  - FOL 准确率: 81.9%, Code 准确率: 62.7%, Oracle: 87.9%\n- **MVP Gate**: 5/5 PASS\n\n### 3.3 Pipeline v2 & v3（10题试点）\n- **v2 (10题)**: 分歧率 0%, 修复率 T:80% B:40%, FOL acc=90%, Code acc=60%\n- **v3 (10题)**: 分歧率 0%, FOL acc=20%, Code acc=60%\n- 小样本试运行，结果不稳定\n\n### 3.4 Pipeline v4 Full（211题，主实验）\n- **模型**: Qwen3-32B-AWQ\n- **数据集**: FOLIO validation, 全部 211 题（去重后 204 题有效）\n- **运行时间**: 2026-05-14 ~ 2026-05-15（含 resume 和 repair 重跑）\n- **关键指标 (all, N=204)**:\n  - 分歧率 C1: 42.2% [95% CI: 35.3%–49.0%]\n  - 诊断率 C2: 87.2% [79.7%–93.9%]\n  - 错误类型 C3: 4 种 (exec_crash, logic_error, negation_error, quantifier_error)\n  - 定向修复优势 C4: +4.7pp (T:69.8% B:65.1%), Fisher p=0.63 (不显著)\n  - 定位准确率 C5(committed): 94.6% (35/37), C5(coverage): 53.0% (35/66)\n- **关键指标 (excl Unknown, N=135)**:\n  - C1: 38.5%, C2: 84.6%, C3: 3种, C4: -1.9pp (不显著), C5(committed): 96.2%\n- **MVP Gate (all)**: 5/5 PASS\n- **分歧不对称性**: FOL 正确 & Code 错 = 56, Code 正确 & FOL 错 = 10 (5.6:1)\n- **准确率**: FOL=78.9%, Code=59.8%, Oracle=87.8%, Pipeline(repair+fallback)=81.4%\n\n### 3.5 GT 子集分析\n| 子集 | N | 分歧率 | 诊断率 | 定向优势 |\n|------|---|--------|--------|----------|\n| GT=True | 72 | 30.6% | 90.9% | -13.6pp |\n| GT=False | 63 | 47.6% | 80.0% | +6.7pp |\n| GT=Unknown | 69 | 49.3% | 91.2% | +14.7pp |\n\n### 3.6 成本效益分析\n- Token 总量: 3,543,447 tokens, 1,196 API calls\n- 每样本平均: 17,370 tokens, 5.9 calls\n- 成本倍数: 全 pipeline / 单表征 = 5.76x\n- 收益: +21.6% 准确率提升 (vs Code-only baseline)\n\n### 3.7 ProofWriter 试点（进行中）\n- **数据集**: ProofWriter OWA-D5, 30 题 pilot\n- **状态**: 已完成 28/30 条（pipeline.log 仍在运行中）\n- **运行时间**: 2026-05-15\n\n## 4. C1-C5 MVP Gate 定义\n\n| Gate | 名称 | 公式 | 阈值 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| C1 | Disagree Rate | FOL判断 ≠ Code判断 / N | ≥10% |\n| C2 | Diagnostic Rate | (≥1方正确) / n_disagree | ≥40% |\n| C3 | Error Type Diversity | 不同错误类型数 | ≥2 |\n| C4 | Targeted vs Blind | targeted_rate − blind_rate | >5pp |\n| C5 | Localization Acc | stability.predicted == GT-actual | ≥60% |\n\n## 5. 已知问题\n\n1. **C4 统计不显著**: Fisher exact test p=0.63，定向修复优势在全集上无统计显著性\n2. **C4 在 excl_unknown 上为负**: -1.9pp，说明对 GT=True/False 样本，定向修复反而略差于盲修\n3. **C5 bug 修复历史**: 原始 C5 计算存在循环论证（predicted 和 actual 都从 GT 派生），已修复为用 stability check\n4. **GT=True 子集定向修复效果差**: 定向优势 -13.6pp，case study 分析表明 GT=True 时 FOL 表征过于自信\n\n## 6. 值得保留的数据\n\n| 文件 | 内容 | 保留价值 |\n|------|------|----------|\n| `results/mvp_v4_full.jsonl` | 204 条完整 pipeline 结果 | 高（主实验原始数据） |\n| `results/eval_unified_folio_d012.json` | 统一评估含 bootstrap CI | 高（统计分析） |\n| `results/exp001_deep_analysis.json/txt` | 错误分布+GT子集分析 | 高（论文素材） |\n| `results/exp001_supplement_ef.json/txt` | phi系数+成本效益 | 高（论文素材） |\n| `results/baseline.json` | 50 题 baseline 原始结果 | 中 |\n| `results/proofwriter_pilot.jsonl` | ProofWriter 28 条结果 | 中（跨数据集泛化） |\n| `artifacts/c1c5_audit.md` | C5 bug 修复审计 | 中（方法论记录） |\n",
        "numLines": 135,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 135
      }
    },

imperative\"(SatLM)。一句重定义格局。\n\n3. **Parse-Plan-Execute分解**（SatLM）：展示offload步骤数(ProgLM 1/3 vs SatLM 2/3)——通用于hybrid系统。\n\n4. **对手优势反转**（SymbCoT）：外部solver的reliability重构为\"inflexibility+information loss\"。\n\n5. **Teaser result图放Intro**（Aristotle）：Fig1 bar chart量化问题，未看方法已知需求。\n\n6. **诊断baseline**（LINC Scratchpad, SatLM CoTSolver）：中间态baseline隔离组件因果贡献。\n\n7. **Mirror figure pair**（Khan Fig3-4）：同指标两设置相反趋势，视觉对称强化论证。\n\n8. **Design space分解**（xiong2024）：empirical study组织为prompting×sampling×aggregation三维框架。\n\n## Figure编排\n\n**Fig1类型**：method_overview(7/11)最多；problem_illustration(3/11)其次；teaser_result(1/11)。\n\n**必配图型**（高）：depth-accuracy曲线(5/11 neurosymbolic必做)；execution rate对比(3/11)。\n\n**叙事节奏**：motivation/problem → method → main_results(bar) → depth(line) → ablation(grouped_bar) → error_analysis。5篇核心论文(LINC/Logic-LM/Aristotle/SymbCoT/SatLM)均遵循。Figure数量5-8常见；Khan极端17图1表。\n\n## Table编排\n\n**主结果表**（8/11，高）：row=methods(by_category分LLM-only/hybrid)，col=datasets。bold_best通用(8/11)；underline_second(3/11)。delta brackets(SymbCoT)新趋势。Baseline 3-10个。\n\n**消融表**：full_numbers(6/11)主流；checkmark_grid(2/11,Aristotle)适合多组件on/off。SatLM增加error type分解表(planning vs execution)——值得借鉴。\n\n**统计报告**：多数single run；SatLM bootstrap p<0.05；CREST mean across 3 seeds。要求正在收紧。\n\n**附录**：prompt templates + extra models + error examples(8/11有)。SatLM 7表最多(含exemplar sensitivity/cross-model)。\n",
      "numLines": 60,

````text
1	---
2	description: 用 PaperBanana 生成高品质学术概念图（架构/流程/framework）。统计图不要用 PaperBanana，用 draw-paper-figure。需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
3	name: paper-banana

6	  - visualization

13	  - request_secret
14	  - poll_secret_request
15	---
16	
17	# Paper Banana — 高品质学术概念图生成
18	
19	PaperBanana 由 Gemini Pro 自动写 matplotlib 代码，VLM Critic 评估渲染结果并迭代优化，输出顶会级别 300 DPI 概念图。
20	
21	**质量底线：每张图必须达到顶会论文的美观度——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。**
22	
23	## 适用范围
24	
25	| 场景 | 用什么 |
26	|------|--------|
27	| **概念图**（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana** ← 本 skill |
28	| **统计图**（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md**。**不要用 PaperBanana 画统计图** |
29	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md** |
30	
31	## 执行环境
32	
33	**PaperBanana 在本机（EC2）运行，不要 SSH 到远程服务器。**
34	
35	EC2（ap-northeast-1 东京）可直接访问 Gemini API，无需代理。
36	
37	## 前置条件
38	
39	### Step 1：获取 GOOGLE_API_KEY
40	
41	```python
42	request_secret(key_id="google_api_key", project="&lt;project_name>", purpose="PaperBanana 概念图生成")
43	# 等待 Director 审批后
44	poll_secret_request(request_id="<返回的 request_id>", project="&lt;project_name>")
45	```
46	
47	如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
48	
49	### Step 2：安装验证（每次使用前必须检查）
50	
51	```bash
52	# 检查是否已安装
53	paperbanana --version 2>/dev/null || {
54	  echo "PaperBanana 未安装，正在安装..."
55	  pip install paperbanana -q
56	  paperbanana --version
57	}
58	```
59	
60	如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。
61	
62	**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 PaperBanana。
63	
64	## 命令：generate
65	
66	概念图用 `generate` 子命令。**必须显式指定 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro`**（默认模型 `gemini-2.0-flash` 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。
67	
68	## 完整流程
69	
70	### Step 1：写意图描述文件
71	
72	将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：
73	
74	```bash
75	cat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'
76	Three-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):
77	
78	Panel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):
79	Grouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).
80	Exact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...
81	Y-axis 'Mid-turn GAR' range 0–0.55. Only left+bottom spines. Serif font 10pt.
82	Add bracket annotation '8-17x gap' between bar groups.
83	
84	Panel (b) 'Diagnostic Chain' (center, ~24% width):
85	Vertical flowchart, 4 rounded boxes connected by thin gray arrows:
86	  Step 1: light gray (#F5F6F7) box, thin border (#DEE2E6), 'Gate Analysis' + subtitle. Green ✓
87	  Step 2: same style, 'PCGrad Exclusion'. Red ✗
88	  Step 3: same style, 'Representation Check'. Red ✗
89	  Step 4: light green (#D4EDDA) background, 'Conclusion: Exposure bias'. Green ✓
90	No axes, no spines. Clean infographic style.
91	
92	Panel (c) 'Constructive Evidence' (right, ~38% width):
93	Line plot. Data points: (0, 0.064), (3, 0.556), (5, 0.727).
94	Navy line (#2C3E50), open circles (white fill, navy edge, width 2).
95	Light blue fill (#EBF5FB, alpha 0.4) under curve.
96	Annotate '11x recovery' in muted gray, 9pt italic.
97	
98	OVERALL STYLE:
99	- 300 DPI, white background, publication quality
100	- Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) throughout
101	- Panel labels '(a)', '(b)', '(c)' in 11pt bold at top-left
102	- Generous white space (wspace=0.35)
103	- Muted academic palette: navy #2C3E50, red #C0392B, green #27AE60, gray #7F8C8D
104	- NO matplotlib default styling: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no tab10 colors
105	- Drop shadows on boxes: subtle, 2px offset, 10% opacity
106	- Rounded corners 8px on all rectangles
107	- Arrow heads: small, elegant, not bulky
108	INTENT
109	```
110	
111	### Step 2：运行 generate
112	
113	```bash
114	GOOGLE_API_KEY="[REDACTED]" paperbanana generate \
115	  --input /tmp/fig_intent.txt \
116	  --caption "Three-panel diagnostic framework: (a) TF vs AR performance gap, (b) systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence" \
117	  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \
118	  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \
119	  --iterations 5
120	```
121	
122	### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）
123	
124	**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：
125	
126	1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？
127	2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？
128	3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？
129	4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？
130	5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？
131	
132	**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**
133	
134	### Step 4：转 PDF
135	
136	```bash
137	python3 -c "
138	import matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')
139	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg
140	img = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')
141	h, w = img.shape[:2]
142	fig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)
143	ax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')
144	plt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)
145	plt.close()
146	"
147	```
148	
149	## 意图描述质量红线
150	
151	Intent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：
152	
153	1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）
154	2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)
155	3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说"蓝色"，说 "#2C3E50"，绑定语义
156	4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation
157	5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels
158	6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度
159	7. **反面约束**："NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors"
160	
161	### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）
162	
163	```
164	STYLE REQUIREMENTS:
165	- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)
166	- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like
167	- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes
168	- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles
169	- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults
170	- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations
171	- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors
172	- Generous white space between elements
173	- Consistent visual weight across all panels
174	```
175	
176	### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）
177	
178	- "Draw a pipeline diagram with three stages"
179	- "Architecture diagram for our system"
180	
181	太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 默认样式填充所有你没指定的细节，结果就是廉价感。
182	
183	## 迭代策略（至少 3 轮外部迭代，Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）
184	
185	PaperBanana 内部有 VLM Critic 迭代（`--iterations 5`），但这还不够。你必须做**外部迭代**：
186	
187	### 第 1 轮：基础生成
188	运行 PaperBanana → Read PNG → 识别大方向问题（布局、配色、结构）
189	
190	### 第 2 轮：结构修复
191	修改意图描述（布局占比、颜色 hex、组件关系）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
192	
193	### 第 3 轮：细节打磨
194	修改意图描述（字号、阴影、对齐、箭头粗细）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
195	
196	### 第 4 轮（Hero Figure）：风格统一
197	线宽 / 字号 / 间距全图统一？与论文其他图配色一致？→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
198	
199	### 第 5 轮（Hero Figure）：最终 polish
200	像素级检查 → 缩到 column width 后文字 ≥ 7pt？→ 终极判断：放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？
201	
202	### 判断标准
203	
204	**可以交付**：看起来像 NeurIPS 2025 best paper 里的图。
205	**不可交付**：看起来像 matplotlib gallery 的示例图。
206	
207	## 失败回退
208	
209	如果 PaperBanana 多次崩溃（API 错误、模型不兼容）：
210	
211	1. 确认 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro` 是否指定
212	2. 确认 GOOGLE_API_KEY 是否有效：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GOOGLE_API_KEY"` 应返回 200
213	3. 如果仍然失败，回退 matplotlib 手写：
214	   - Read `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 获取 L1-L4 设计标准
215	   - 写 Python 脚本手动画概念图（FancyBboxPatch、FancyArrowPatch 等）
216	   - **同样必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮**
217	   - **同样必须达到顶会级别的美观度**
218	   - 保存 .py 脚本到 figures/paper/ 目录便于后续修改
219	
````

\"Architecture diagram for our system\"\n\n太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 默认样式填充所有你没指定的细节，结果就是廉价感。\n\n## 迭代策略（至少 3 轮外部迭代，Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）\n\nPaperBanana 内部有 VLM Critic 迭代（`--iterations 5`），但这还不够。你必须做**外部迭代**：\n\n### 第 1 轮：基础生成\n运行 PaperBanana → Read PNG → 识别大方向问题（布局、配色、结构）\n\n### 第 2 轮：结构修复\n修改意图描述（布局占比、颜色 hex、组件关系）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 3 轮：细节打磨\n修改意图描述（字号、阴影、对齐、箭头粗细）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 4 轮（Hero Figure）：风格统一\n线宽 / 字号 / 间距全图统一？与论文其他图配色一致？→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 5 轮（Hero Figure）：最终 polish\n像素级检查 → 缩到 column width 后文字 ≥ 7pt？→ 终极判断：放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n\n### 判断标准\n\n**可以交付**：看起来像 NeurIPS 2025 best paper 里的图。\n**不可交付**：看起来像 matplotlib gallery 的示例图。\n\n## 失败回退\n\n如果 PaperBanana 多次崩溃（API 错误、模型不兼容）：\n\n1. 确认 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro` 是否指定\n2. 确认 GOOGLE_API_KEY 是否有效：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' \"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GOOGLE_API_KEY\"` 应返回 200\n3. 如果仍然失败，回退 matplotlib 手写：\n   - Read `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 获取 L1-L4 设计标准\n   - 写 Python 脚本手动画概念图（FancyBboxPatch、FancyArrowPatch 等）\n   - **同样必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮**\n   - **同样必须达到顶会级别的美观度**\n   - 保存 .py 脚本到 figures/paper/ 目录便于后续修改\n",

_source line 815 · 2026-05-24T19:34:57.169Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01DBcEmGPNH3VJdKQUM9YL7A`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	description: 学术论文图表设计与生成——四层指南（防丑原则 + 叙事原则 + Pattern 库 + 工具路由）。Paper Agent 规划 visualization_plan 时读，Worker 执行时也读。
3	name: paper-visualization
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	- research
7	- visualization
8	- paper
9	- matplotlib
10	- paperbanana
11	version: 5
12	roles:
13	- agent
14	- paper_agent
15	---
16	
17	# Paper Visualization — 学术图表设计与生成
18	
19	> **核心理念**：好图不是"画得对"，是"回答一个问题"。每张图动笔前先问：**这张图要让读者看到什么？**
20	>
21	> **质量底线**：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。
22	
23	本 skill 分五层：
24	
25	- **L0 学习同行** — 设计前看几张优秀同类型论文图，比读任何原则都有效
26	- **L1 防丑原则** — 底线，所有图必须遵守
27	- **L2 叙事原则** — 让图有说服力（区分"数据图"和"论证图"）
28	- **L3 Pattern 库** — 具体技法目录，按 `design_notes` 选用
29	- **L4 工具路由 + 模板** — paperbanana vs matplotlib，常用统计图模板
30	
31	---
32	
33	## L0 学习同行（设计前的功课）
34	
35	你的子领域里，**最被引用、最被记住的论文是怎么画图的？** 在动笔之前花点时间去看一眼——这个动作的回报远高于读完下面的 L1-L4。
36	
37	### 为什么这一步是重要的
38	
39	- 同领域读者已经被这些论文的视觉语言"训练"过——遵循惯例 = 降低读者认知负担
40	- 顶会论文经历过 reviewer 严苛审查，能登出来的图基本说明它的 narrative 设计是被接受的
41	- 抽象原则告诉你"用 fill_between 表示发散"，但好论文告诉你"在这种数据上，发散到底应该画多淡才合适"
42	- 一个领域往往有它特有的图表惯例（如校准研究的 reliability diagram、scaling 研究的 log-log curve、RL 的 learning curve with seed bands），不看就不会用
43	
44	### 什么时候值得投入
45	
46	判断权交给你。一些可参考的信号：
47	
48	- **值得多花时间**：会决定论文成败的图（hero figure、main result、新颖的实验类型）
49	- **不太值得**：常规 supplementary 图、loss curve 这类标准化已经很高的图
50	- **强烈建议看**：你打算画一种自己不熟悉的图，或者数据形态是这个领域少见的
51	
52	### 怎么看
53	
54	工具：`paper-image-extractor` skill 可以从 arXiv source 或 PDF 提取参考论文里的 figure。你已经有 `literature_map.yaml` 里的论文清单作为起点。
55	
56	看的时候问自己（不是机械检查清单，是真的去理解）：
57	
58	- 这张图回答了什么问题？读者一眼能 get 到吗？
59	- 它用了哪些叙事层（background band / 参考线 / 概念曲线 / fill 区域）？这些层在我的数据上有对应物吗？
60	- 它的颜色和 marker 怎么承载语义？我的图能不能借鉴这套映射？
61	- 它有什么是这篇论文特有的（与他们的具体数据 / claim 绑死）？这些不要照搬。
62	- 如果让我重画它的图，我会怎么改？为什么作者没这么做？
63	
64	### 怎么把学到的传下去
65	
66	把你看完后的判断**直接写进对应 fig 的 `design_notes`**，用人话不用模板。例子：
67	
68	```yaml
69	design_notes:
70	  - "学 GPTQ Fig 4：用 log-scale bit-width + dashed FP16 baseline + 不同 marker shape 分模型族"
71	  - "Pattern A（regime band）划分 in-domain / OOD 两段"
72	  - "AWQ Fig 2 那种 hatching 区分 outlier 不适用我们的数据，跳过"
73	```
74	
75	Worker 拿到这种 design_notes 就知道既要参考 GPTQ 的 layout 又要用 Pattern A，能直接落实成代码。
76	
77	### 边界
78	
79	- 不要复刻，要消化。直接抄一张图既没用（数据不一样）也有风险（与他们的 claim 强绑定）
80	- 不要看太多。3 张就够，多了 Paper Agent 会陷入"该听谁的"
81	- 看不到原图（提取失败 / arXiv 无 source）就跳过，不要在这里卡住流程
82	
83	---
84	
85	## L1 防丑原则（底线，不可违反）
86	
87	来自 Tufte 和 Luís Cruz 16 条公认规范。Agent 默认遵守，无需逐条思考。
88	
89	1. **Self-explanatory** — 不看正文就能看懂：title/caption + 所有轴有 label + 单位
90	2. **去 chartjunk** — 不要 3D、不要背景框、不要无意义网格、不要 dual y-axis、不要 pie/donut chart
91	3. **直接标注 > Legend** — 能直接标在曲线/柱旁就不用 legend；legend 必要时不遮数据
92	4. **颜色/形状/纹理只用于 data variation** — 不用颜色装饰，不用相同颜色表示不同数据
93	5. **Meaningful baseline** — bar chart 必须从 0 起；scatter 可以非 0
94	6. **文字水平** — label 不要旋转/垂直；类别名太长 → 用 horizontal bar chart
95	7. **Sort for comparison** — 柱状图按数值排序，便于对比（除非有自然顺序如时间）
96	8. **字号一致** — 用 mm 计算 figsize（`ONE_MM = 1/25.4`），不在 LaTeX 里 `\includegraphics[scale=...]` resize
97	9. **色盲友好** — 默认 Okabe-Ito；红/绿区分时变 saturation；考虑 grayscale 打印
98	10. **Bold 只用于强调** — 不要全图加粗；highlight 留给 takeaway
99	11. **Line plot 只用于有序数据** — 类别变量用 bar
100	12. **单 x 单 y 轴** — 必须双轴时改成 multi-panel
101	13. **300 DPI、PDF 矢量、savefig.bbox=tight、去掉 top/right spines**
102	
103	---
104	
105	## L2 叙事原则（让图变成论证）
106	
107	L1 让图"不丑"，L2 让图"有说服力"。这一层是 Agent 自主画图时最容易缺失的。
108	
109	### 原则 1：每张图回答一个明确问题
110	
111	动笔前用一句话写下："This figure shows that ___."
112	
113	如果写不出来，就不要画。如果写出来的 takeaway 不是论文的 claim，就要重新设计。
114	
115	**反例**：`"Accuracy across methods"` — 这只是个标题，不是问题
116	**正例**：`"FP16 baseline 在小模型上比量化版高 5pp，但 70B 模型上差距消失"` — 这是一张图要说服读者相信的事
117	
118	`visualization_plan.{fig_id}.narrative_intent` 字段写这一句。
119	
120	### 原则 2：颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序
121	
122	错的做法：`colors = ['blue', 'orange', 'green', 'red']` 按数据顺序分配。
123	对的做法：`BN → 蓝色（始终）`、`non-BN → 橙色（始终）`、`collapse/danger → vermillion (#D55E00)`、`safe/baseline → blue (#0072B2)`。
124	
125	整篇论文 6 张图，BN 永远蓝、collapse 永远红——零认知负担，读者一眼建立映射。
126	
127	### 原则 3：信息层级用 visual variable 表达
128	
129	数据的"质量"和"分类"应该可见，不应该藏在 caption 里：
130	
131	| 信息 | Visual 表达 |
132	|------|------------|
133	| n < 50 低置信度点 | hollow marker + 标注 `n=` |
134	| Out-of-sample / pending 数据 | hollow + 不同 marker shape（如 diamond）+ "OOS" label |
135	| Collapse / 异常类别 | hatching（斜线填充）|
136	| 主结果 vs 次要结果 | 主用饱和色，次要用 muted 色或 alpha |
137	
138	### 原则 4：加叙事层，不只画数据
139	
140	数据是图的基础，叙事层是让读者"读懂"的关键。常见叙事层：
141	
142	- **Background bands** — `axhspan/axvspan` 标定性区域（"collapse zone"、"safe zone"、"Regime A/B/C"）
143	- **Fill_between** — 显示两条曲线发散区域（alpha 极低如 0.06，不抢眼但有面积）
144	- **Reference lines** — 锚定真实值（如 dashed line 标"actual W3A16 accuracy"）
145	- **Equivalence band** — `axvspan` gradient fade 标 +/-1pp 等价区间
146	- **Concept curve** — 完全合成的曲线（如 cosine trajectory）建立理论直觉
147	
148	这些都不来自数据，但帮读者"看到"数据想说什么。
149	
150	### 原则 5：Subtitle / panel label 写 insight 不写描述
151	
152	错：`"Layer 6 comparison across three models"`
153	对：`"Same relative position, opposite function"`
154	
155	错：`"Reliability diagram"`
156	对：`"Quantization shifts confidence but not accuracy"`
157	
158	副标题是免费的 takeaway，不要浪费。
159	
160	### 原则 6：Multi-panel 每个面板独立回答一个 sub-question
161	
162	不要做"主图 + 装饰小图"。每个 panel 必须有独立的信息价值。
163	
164	如果一个 panel 想不出独立的问题，要么删掉，要么合并到主 panel。
165	
166	布局上：非对称比例（如 1.4:1）让视觉重心对齐叙事重心。
167	
168	### 原则 7：所有图至少 3 轮迭代，Hero figure 至少 5 轮
169	
170	第一版画出来基本不能直接用。计划好预算：先确定数据和布局，再调样式标注，最后 polish。不要指望一次到位。
171	
172	### Hero Figure 定义
173	
174	Hero Figure = Figure 1 + 主结果图（main result figure）。这两类图对论文第一印象影响最大，必须投入更多迭代。
175	
176	---
177	
178	## L3 Pattern 库（按 `design_notes` 选用）
179	
180	按"图要回答的问题"选 pattern。每个 pattern 给出**何时用**、**怎么用**（具体代码或 matplotlib API）。
181	
182	### Pattern A：定性区域 / regime bands
183	
184	**何时用**：x 或 y 轴有自然分段（regime、phase、severity tier）。
185	
186	```python
187	# horizontal bands
188	for lo, hi, color, label in [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'), (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B')]:
189	    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)
190	    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center', fontsize=9, color='gray')
191	
192	# 单一 collapse zone
193	ax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)
194	ax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8, color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic')
195	```
196	
197	### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between
198	
199	**何时用**：两组数据从某点开始发散，想让"发散程度"可见。
200	
201	```python
202	# alpha 0.06 极淡，不抢眼但有面积
203	ax.fill_between(x_range, curve_high, curve_low, alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1)
204	```
205	
206	### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值
207	
208	**何时用**：合成 / 缩放后的 x 轴需要锚定到真实物理量。
209	
210	```python
211	ax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3)
212	t = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value+1, 'actual W3A16', fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5)
213	t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])  # 防遮挡
214	```
215	
216	### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid）
217	
218	**何时用**：同一图中有不同置信度 / OOS / pending 数据。
219	
220	```python
221	# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心 = n < 50
222	if n < 50:
223	    ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,
224	            markeredgewidth=1.2, markersize=6)
225	    ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), xytext=(0, 7), textcoords='offset points',
226	                fontsize=5, color=color, fontstyle='italic')
227	```
228	
229	### Pattern E：Wilson CI / bootstrap 不确定性
230	
231	**何时用**：accuracy / proportion 数据需要置信区间。
232	
233	```python
234	def wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):
235	    denom = 1 + z**2 / n
236	    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom
237	    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom
238	    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)
239	```
240	
241	### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（解决标注重叠）
242	
243	**何时用**：scatter 上多个点需要标注且自动布局重叠。
244	
245	```python
246	label_offsets = {
247	    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),   # 向下避让
248	    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),   # 向左避让
249	    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),
250	}
251	for name, (x, y) in data.items():
252	    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))
253	    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy), textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7)
254	```
255	
256	### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度
257	
258	**何时用**：已用颜色表示一个维度，再加一个二元维度（如 collapse vs not）。
259	
260	```python
261	for is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):
262	    if is_collapse:
263	        bar.set_hatch('///')
264	        bar.set_alpha(0.7)
265	```
266	
267	### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局
268	
269	**何时用**：主图 + 辅助 panel；或两个等重要 panel 但视觉重心需要倾斜。
270	
271	```python
272	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),
273	                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})
274	# 主面板 1.4x 宽，副面板 1x；视觉重心在左
275	```
276	
277	### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）
278	
279	**何时用**：数据范围跨度大，或对数轴需要表示 0。
280	
281	```python
282	d = 0.012
283	kwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black', clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)
284	ax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)  # 右下角斜线
285	ax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)  # 右上角斜线
286	```
287	
288	### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边
289	
290	**何时用**：标注必须放在数据点 / colored region 上方。
291	
292	```python
293	import matplotlib.patheffects as pe
294	t = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')
295	t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])
296	```
297	
298	### Pattern K：Concept curve（建立直觉）
299	
300	**何时用**：论文核心 claim 是定性现象，需要先让读者理解理论预期。
301	
302	```python
303	# 完全合成的 cosine trajectory，不是实验数据
304	x = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)
305	trajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2
306	ax.plot(x, trajectory, linewidth=2, solid_capstyle='round')
307	ax.annotate('gap eliminated', (1.0, final_y), color='#388E3C',
308	            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9)
309	```
310	
311	### Pattern L：Forest plot（meta-analysis 风格）
312	
313	**何时用**：多个子条件的 effect size + CI 对比，加 inline 统计数字。
314	
315	3-column GridSpec：(1) 标签 (2) CI 区间图 (3) 统计数字（mean, CI, p-value）
316	
317	每行：dot + horizontal line(CI) + 数字 inline。等价带用半透明 axvspan。
318	
319	### 概念图 L1 防丑原则（7 条）
320	
321	概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意）的视觉底线，与统计图 L1 互补。
322	
323	1. **Flow direction 一致** — 全图统一左→右或上→下，不混合方向
324	2. **Box 规格统一** — 同层级 box 尺寸、圆角、边框粗细一致
325	3. **箭头样式统一** — 不混用多种箭头类型（除非有语义区分）
326	4. **层级清晰** — 不同抽象层用缩进/嵌套/背景色区分
327	5. **密度控制** — 单张图 ≤15 个 box，超过则拆分或分组
328	6. **Label 完整** — 每个 box 和箭头都有文字说明
329	7. **无装饰性元素** — 不加纯装饰图标、渐变、3D 效果
330	
331	### 概念图 L2 叙事原则（6 条）
332	
333	让概念图不只是"画了个框图"，而是"引导读者理解系统"。
334	
335	1. **视觉锚点** — 核心贡献用饱和色/大字/加粗边框突出，其余 muted
336	2. **阅读路径** — 沿箭头方向自然读完全图，不需要跳跃
337	3. **对比布局** — 左右并列展示 before/after 或 baseline/ours
338	4. **分组嵌套** — 用浅色背景框将相关组件分组（如 encoder/decoder）
339	5. **箭头语义** — 实线=数据流，虚线=控制流/可选，粗线=关键路径
340	6. **信息层级** — 主路径用饱和色+大字，辅助信息用灰色+小字
341	
342	### 概念图 L3 Pattern 库
343	
344	| Pattern | 名称 | 适用场景 |
345	|---------|------|---------|
346	| P-A | 线性流程 | 数据处理 pipeline、推理流程 |
347	| P-B | 层级嵌套 | 模型架构（encoder/decoder/attention） |
348	| P-C | 对比并列 | baseline vs ours、before/after |
349	| P-D | 分支汇聚 | 多输入融合、ensemble |
350	| P-E | 循环 | 训练循环、迭代优化 |
351	| P-F | 诊断链 | 消融实验逻辑、排错流程 |
352	| P-G | 三面板综合 | 概念+数据+结论（常用于 Figure 1） |
353	
354	### 概念图 Checklist（7 项）
355	
356	- [ ] Flow direction 全图统一（左→右或上→下）
357	- [ ] Box 规格一致（同层级尺寸、圆角、边框相同）
358	- [ ] 视觉层级清晰（核心突出，辅助 muted）
359	- [ ] 核心贡献在视觉上最突出
360	- [ ] 箭头语义一致（实线/虚线/粗细有意义）
361	- [ ] 留白充足（box 之间不拥挤）
362	- [ ] 跨图风格一致（配色、字号、线宽与论文其他图统一）
363	
364	---
365	
366	## L4 工具路由 + 模板
367	
368	### 路由规则
369	
370	| 场景 | 工具 | 原因 |
371	|------|------|------|
372	| 概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana（Gemini）** | matplotlib 手写 boxes/arrows 极痛苦 |
373	| 统计图（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **matplotlib** | 确定性、可控、有模板 |
374	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **matplotlib** | 不依赖外部 API |
375	
376	项目级偏好：`config/projects/{name}.yaml` 的 `visualization.preferred_tool` 可覆盖。
377	
378	### Matplotlib 通用样式（rcParams 标准）
379	
380	```python
381	import matplotlib
382	matplotlib.use('Agg')
383	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
384	import matplotlib.patheffects as pe
385	import numpy as np
386	
387	plt.rcParams.update({
388	    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',
389	    'font.size': 11, 'axes.titlesize': 13, 'axes.labelsize': 12,
390	    'xtick.labelsize': 10, 'ytick.labelsize': 10, 'legend.fontsize': 10,
391	    'figure.dpi': 300, 'savefig.dpi': 300,
392	    'savefig.bbox': 'tight', 'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,
393	    'axes.spines.top': False, 'axes.spines.right': False,
394	    'pdf.fonttype': 42, 'ps.fonttype': 42,  # TrueType 嵌入
395	    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,
396	})
397	
398	# 色盲友好语义色板（Okabe-Ito）
399	SEMANTIC_COLORS = {
400	    'baseline':   '#0072B2',  # blue - safe / mild
401	    'treatment':  '#E69F00',  # orange - intervention
402	    'collapse':   '#D55E00',  # vermillion - danger / catastrophic
403	    'safe':       '#009E73',  # green - good outcome
404	    'neutral':    '#999999',  # gray - reference / control
405	}
406	```
407	
408	### 常用模板（仅作起点，需按 narrative_intent 改造）
409	
410	详细执行模板见 `skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`：
411	- Reliability Diagram（校准图）
412	- ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）
413	- T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）
414	
415	**重要**：这些模板是起点不是终点。每张图根据 `narrative_intent` 必须加 L3 中的相关 pattern，不能直接套用。
416	
417	### 主图（Figure 1）特别提示
418	
419	大多数 ML 论文的 Figure 1 是概念图/架构图/方法概览——正是 PaperBanana 的强项。除非 Figure 1 是纯数值结果对比（如 Pareto frontier），否则优先用 PaperBanana。Figure 1 的 `narrative_intent` 必须与 `writing_plan.yaml` 的 `meta.core_claim` 直接关联。
420	
421	### PaperBanana 用法
422	
423	见 `skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`。意图描述时直接引用 `narrative_intent`，并把 L3 中的 pattern 名写进 intent（如 "with collapse zone background band, hollow markers for n<50"）。
424	
425	---
426	
427	## 给 Paper Agent 的填写规则
428	
429	规划 `visualization_plan` 时，对每张图填：
430	
431	```yaml
432	- fig_id: fig2_main_result
433	  type: statistical
434	  tool: matplotlib
435	  data_source: "registry: exp_001, exp_002"
436	  placement: experiments
437	
438	  # 新字段（必填）
439	  narrative_intent: "Quantized models match FP16 on accuracy but degrade calibration; ECE gap widens with bits→2."
440	  design_notes:
441	    - "Pattern A: regime bands for bits=8/4/3"
442	    - "Pattern C: dashed line for FP16 ECE baseline"
443	    - "Pattern D: hollow markers for n<50 data points"
444	
445	  aspect_ratio: "4:3"
446	```
447	
448	`narrative_intent`：一句话，主动语态，描述读者应该相信的事。
449	
450	`design_notes`：从 L3 选 0-4 个 pattern，给出选用理由。可以为空（说明默认模板足够）。
451	
452	---
453	
454	## 提交前 Checklist（以下为统计图专用）
455	
456	L1 部分（防丑底线）：
457	- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位
458	- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie
459	- [ ] 颜色绑定语义、色盲友好
460	- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（如有）
461	- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）
462	- [ ] PDF 矢量、300 DPI
463	
464	L2 部分（叙事强度）：
465	- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）
466	- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight，不是描述
467	- [ ] 数据质量差异在视觉上可见（n、OOS、pending 等）
468	- [ ] 至少考虑过是否需要 background band / fill_between / reference line（不一定要加）
469	
470	L3 部分（高级感——不可跳过）：
471	- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）
472	- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼，不过度饱和
473	- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一
474	- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐）
475	- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤）
476	- [ ] 与论文全文其他图的风格一致
477	- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？
478	
````
